+- **বার্তা:** fix: initialize litellm proxy settings to fix swarm orchestrator test

+- LLMGateway এ litellm.use_litellm_proxy = False যোগ করা হয়েছে

 from unittest.mock import patch, AsyncMock
+import litellm
 from core.swarm_orchestrator import SwarmOrchestrator
 
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def setup_litellm():
+    """কনফিগার করুন litellm সেটিংস প্রতিটি টেস্টের আগে"""

+    litellm.use_litellm_proxy = False
+    litellm.drop_params = True
+    litellm.telemetry = False
+    yield
+
+
 @pytest.mark.anyio
 async def test_swarm_orchestrator_runs_entire_graph():
     orchestrator = SwarmOrchestrator()
